Act as the user against the real running product - run every flow test, walk the most important flows by hand, sweep the navigation, capture console, network and server errors, classify each failure, and write a per-flow report with evidence and a READY / NOT READY / CANNOT TELL verdict. Reports only; never fixes. Use after uf-test, or in recheck mode after the builder has fixed things. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-evaluate))
- **uf-fix**: Fix what a user-flow or security audit found, one finding at a time - reproduce it with the flow's test, make the smallest fix, prove the flow now works for real, check nothing else broke, and record it. Use after uf-run when asked to fix, resolve or repair the findings, or when given a report.md or flows.yaml with failures. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-fix))
- **uf-flows**: Phase 3 of user-flow QA. Turn the surface map into the full list of user flows - what each kind of user comes to do, every route to each goal, and the variants (wrong input, not allowed, interrupted, empty state, return visit) - written as user actions and what the user should see. Use after uf-discover, or when asked to list user journeys, user flows, scenarios or acceptance cases for a product. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-flows))
- **uf-parallel**: Test a long flow list (hundreds of flows) by splitting it into batches - helper agents in parallel where the harness has them, or several sessions one after another - each with its own test data, with one lead that merges results into the registry and keeps going until every flow is tested. Use in uf-run when the catalogue has more flows than one session can finish, or when asked to continue an audit. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-parallel))
- **uf-patterns**: What actually breaks for users, and how to see it. A catalogue of the ways products fail between frontend, backend, database, background jobs, email and MCP tools, each with the check that exposes it by using the product. Use when checking whether something works, before calling a feature done, when asked to find what is broken, and when writing user-flow tests. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-patterns))
- **uf-preflight**: Phase 1 of user-flow QA. Check the software itself is alive before testing any user flow - install, build, typecheck, existing tests, boot the whole stack with seed data, check every service responds, and check the testing tools work. Use at the start of uf-run, or on its own when asked whether a project builds, starts or runs. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-preflight))
- **uf-ready-check**: Quick check by the session that built or changed something, before it says done, ready, working, fixed or complete. Starts the real product and walks the user flows the change touches, the way a user would, and reports what was verified by use and what was not. Use at the end of any build, feature or fix, before claiming it works. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-ready-check))
- **uf-run**: Check whether a product actually works for the people who use it. Use when asked if a project is ready, done, working, shippable or safe to release; when asked to evaluate, QA, review or find what is broken; or after an agent claims a build is complete. Runs the product for real and tests every user flow end to end (frontend, backend, database, MCP), instead of reviewing code. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-run))
- **uf-score**: Score a finished audit against a list of problems the human already knows about - what it found, what it missed, false alarms, how each finding was known - and add a row to the scorecard. Use after an audit when asked to score, grade or compare runs. Never in the session that did the audit, and never before it finishes. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-score))
- **uf-security**: Security as user flows - check what each kind of user, API key and AI agent can do that they must not, and what leaks out through side doors. Covers access control, tenant isolation, agent and key privileges, audit trails, data egress, injection, secrets and privacy, each checked by using the product as the least-trusted caller, or traced in code when it cannot run. Use for a security review, before release, or with uf-run. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-security))
- **uf-setup**: Set up user-flow QA in the current project - creates the .userflow folder (registry, guide, helper scripts), adds the readiness rules to CLAUDE.md or AGENTS.md, and can enable the plugin for every session on this repo, including cloud sessions. Use when asked to set up or install user-flow QA, or when uf-run finds no .userflow folder. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-setup))
- **uf-test**: Phase 4 of user-flow QA. Write one end-to-end test per user flow that drives the real running product the way a user would (browser, API, MCP client, CLI) and checks what the user sees, what was saved and any side effects - no mocks. Use after uf-flows, or when asked to write user-flow, end-to-end, acceptance or journey tests from a list of flows. ([paarths-collab/user-flow-qa](https://github.com/paarths-collab/user-flow-qa/tree/HEAD/skills/uf-test))
- **ufawkes-knowledge**: Use when searching uFawkesAI institutional knowledge via qmd — agent orchestration, AI-SDLC pipeline, skill/registry conventions, DORA measurement — or when an answer should come from this repo's docs rather than its code. ([paruff/uFawkesAI](https://github.com/paruff/uFawkesAI/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ufawkes-knowledge) · ★2)
- **ufawkesobs-knowledge**: Use when searching uFawkesObs institutional knowledge — architecture rationale, ADRs, runbooks, DORA/GitOps/deployment decisions, past incidents, or when an answer should come from this repo's docs rather than its code. ([paruff/uFawkesObs](https://github.com/paruff/uFawkesObs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ufawkesobs-knowledge))

Use when the user asks about NAS health, storage, RAID, btrfs, USB drives, Docker, systemd services, logs, network ports, or restarting a confirmed service or container. ([jakkzz/agent-skills](https://github.com/jakkzz/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ugreen-nas-ops))
- **ugs-pcb-release**: Use when the user invokes ugs-pcb-release as an alias for unreal-ugs-release. ([rhyolyn/handbag-of-holding](https://github.com/rhyolyn/handbag-of-holding/tree/HEAD/secret-agents/docs/migrations/2026-10-13-spec-recovery/.superseded-directories/ugs-pcb-release))
- **ugsci-roles**: 当使用 spawn_subagent（单次或 batch 模式）分派子 agent 时，根据子 agent 的角色设置相应的 allowed_tools 和 skills 参数。 ([Adair-Shuai/QwenPaw](https://github.com/Adair-Shuai/QwenPaw/tree/HEAD/plugins/bundle/ugsci/skills/ugsci-roles) · 2 copies)
- **ugui-from-screenshot**: UI 디자인을 기반으로 UGUI를 자동 구성하는 스킬. Figma URL은 UnityToFigma 패키지로 일괄 포팅한 뒤 부족한 부분만 unity-cli로 보정하고, 스크린샷은 비전+unity-cli로 구성한다. 라운드 코너는 자동 보정하지 않고 사용자에게 보고한다. 'UGUI from screenshot', 'UGUI from Figma', '스크린샷으로 UI', 'UI 구현', 'screenshot to UGUI', '스크린샷 기반 UI', 'Figma로 UGUI', 'Figma UI 구현' 등의 요청 시 트리거된다. ([geuneda/ugui-from-screenshot](https://github.com/geuneda/ugui-from-screenshot/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★1)
- **uhandler**: First-line functional module with single responsibility logic. Distinguishes Handler (has logic, communicates with Manager) from EventListener (only listens, exposes UnityEvents). ([juanFrancoSalcedo/NW-Fedearroz-2026](https://github.com/juanFrancoSalcedo/NW-Fedearroz-2026/tree/HEAD/skills/uhandler))
- **uhd-kanban**: 프로젝트의 칸반 보드(`kanban/` 의 카드 `.md` 파일)를 읽고 고친다. 카드 만들기 · 열 옮기기 · 순서 바꾸기 · 코멘트 · 아카이브 · 카드를 `claude --bg` 세션으로 실행하기 · 멈춘 세션 이어 가기. "칸반", "카드 만들어", "k123 옮겨", "보드 정리", "카드 실행", "inbox 확인" 같은 요청이거나 프로젝트 루트에 `.uhd-kanban/` 이 있으면 연다. English triggers: "kanban", "create a card", "move k123", "clean up the board", "run the card", "check inbox", "migrate the board". ([hwanyong/skill-kanban](https://github.com/hwanyong/skill-kanban/tree/HEAD/plugins/uhd-kanban/skills/uhd-kanban))
- **uheg**: UHEg项目模板仓库的正文写作与编译规范:make 编译、内容/ 章节与独立页入口、配置.typ(导入/导航/元素数据/网页模板)、元素系统 CSV、中文模板函数与站点参数、Markdown 导入、Git LFS 图片、网页发布、白描直陈语法与常见坑。在写或改 内容/ 下 .typ、配置.typ、附件/元素系统.csv,或编译、发布网页时调用。 ([kych-net/HPD](https://github.com/kych-net/HPD/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · 5 copies)
- **uhi-ambulance**: Use when building, debugging or testing UHI Ambulance Booking: finding an ambulance for a pickup, and getting a quote with its terms. Carries the journeys as loops, the calls with their signing, the screen rules, the symptoms of a failed call, and the go-live checks, in references/. ([nha-in/docs](https://github.com/nha-in/docs/tree/HEAD/plugins/uhi-integrators-assistant/skills/uhi-ambulance) · ★1)
- **uhi-ambulance-build**: Use when scaffolding UHI Ambulance Booking: builds each journey as an observe-orient-decide-act loop against the sandbox. ([nha-in/docs](https://github.com/nha-in/docs/tree/HEAD/skills-src/uhi-ambulance-build) · ★1)
